-- tilt
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local M = math
local RAD = M.rad
local ANG = CFrame.Angles
local V3 = Vector3
local V2 = Vector2
local MOVE_TILT_MAX = RAD(65)
local SIT_TILT_MAX = RAD(5)
local VERTICAL_UP_TILT = RAD(35)
local VERTICAL_DOWN_TILT = RAD(10)
local SLOPE_MAX = RAD(45)
local SMOOTH = 10

local LeanConfig = {
	LeftLean = 0.5,
	RightLean = 0.5,
	WalkLean = 0.5,
	RunLean = 0.8,
	BackwardsLean = 0.5,
	JumpLean = 1,
	FallLean = 1
}

local IdleConfig = {
	Enabled = true,
	IdleThreshold = 0.1,
	RotationX = RAD(0),
	RotationY = RAD(0),
	RotationZ = RAD(0),
	Smooth = 8
}

local function lerpAngle(a, b, t)
	return a + M.atan2(M.sin(b - a), M.cos(b - a)) * t
end

local isShiftLock = false
local inputConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
	if gp then return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then
		isShiftLock = not isShiftLock
	end
end)

local renderConnection = nil
local transparencyConnection = nil

local function isBlockDisabled()
	local part = Workspace:FindFirstChild("INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE")
	if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
		if not transparencyConnection then
			transparencyConnection = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function() end)
		end
		return part.Transparency >= 1
	else
		if transparencyConnection then
			transparencyConnection:Disconnect()
			transparencyConnection = nil
		end
		return false
	end
end

local function cleanup(hrp, attachment, align)
	if renderConnection then
		renderConnection:Disconnect()
		renderConnection = nil
	end
	if transparencyConnection then
		transparencyConnection:Disconnect()
		transparencyConnection = nil
	end
	if attachment then attachment:Destroy() end
	if align then align:Destroy() end
	if hrp and hrp.Parent then
		local hum = hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
	end
end

local function setupCharacter(character)
	if renderConnection then
		renderConnection:Disconnect()
		renderConnection = nil
	end

	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
	local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
	if not humanoid or not hrp then return end

	humanoid.AutoRotate = false

	local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Name = "TiltAttachment"
	attachment.Parent = hrp

	local align = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
	align.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
	align.Attachment0 = attachment
	align.MaxTorque = math.huge
	align.Responsiveness = 50
	align.Parent = hrp

	local lastPos = hrp.Position
	local tiltX, tiltY, tiltZ = 0, 0, 0
	local slopeTilt = 0
	local currentYaw = RAD(hrp.Orientation.Y)
	local idleX, idleY, idleZ = 0, 0, 0

	local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
	rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}

	local diedConnection
	diedConnection = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		cleanup(hrp, attachment, align)
		if diedConnection then diedConnection:Disconnect() end
	end)

	renderConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then 
			cleanup(hrp, attachment, align)
			return 
		end

		if isBlockDisabled() then
			humanoid.AutoRotate = true
			align.Enabled = false
			return
		else
			humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			align.Enabled = true
		end

		local velocity = (hrp.Position - lastPos) / M.max(dt, 1/60)
		lastPos = hrp.Position

		local targetSlope = 0
		local ray = Workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, V3.new(0, -10, 0), rayParams)

		if ray and ray.Instance and ray.Instance.CanCollide then
			targetSlope = -hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(ray.Normal) * SLOPE_MAX * 1.5
		end

		if humanoid.Sit then
			targetSlope *= 2
		end

		slopeTilt += (targetSlope - slopeTilt) * dt * SMOOTH

		local targetX, targetZ = 0, 0
		local moveDir = humanoid.MoveDirection
		local planarSpeed = V2.new(velocity.X, velocity.Z).Magnitude

		local targetIdleX, targetIdleY, targetIdleZ = 0, 0, 0

		if IdleConfig.Enabled and planarSpeed < IdleConfig.IdleThreshold then
			targetIdleX = IdleConfig.RotationX
			targetIdleY = IdleConfig.RotationY
			targetIdleZ = IdleConfig.RotationZ
		end

		idleX += (targetIdleX - idleX) * dt * IdleConfig.Smooth
		idleY += (targetIdleY - idleY) * dt * IdleConfig.Smooth
		idleZ += (targetIdleZ - idleZ) * dt * IdleConfig.Smooth

		local baseTilt = humanoid.Sit and SIT_TILT_MAX or MOVE_TILT_MAX

		if planarSpeed > 0.1 then
			local forwardDot = moveDir:Dot(hrp.CFrame.LookVector)
			local moveMultiplier = (planarSpeed < 16) and LeanConfig.WalkLean or LeanConfig.RunLean

			if forwardDot < 0 then
				moveMultiplier *= LeanConfig.BackwardsLean
			end

			targetX = -forwardDot * baseTilt * moveMultiplier

			local rightDot = moveDir:Dot(hrp.CFrame.RightVector)
			if rightDot > 0 then
				targetZ = -rightDot * baseTilt * LeanConfig.RightLean
			else
				targetZ = -rightDot * baseTilt * LeanConfig.LeftLean
			end
		end

		local intensity = M.clamp(velocity.Y / 50, -1, 1)
		local targetY

		if velocity.Y > 0 then
			targetY = intensity * VERTICAL_UP_TILT * LeanConfig.JumpLean
		else
			targetY = intensity * VERTICAL_DOWN_TILT * LeanConfig.FallLean
		end

		if humanoid.Sit then
			targetY *= 2
		end

		tiltX += (targetX - tiltX) * dt * SMOOTH
		tiltY += (targetY - tiltY) * dt * SMOOTH
		tiltZ += (targetZ - tiltZ) * dt * SMOOTH

		local targetYaw = currentYaw

		if isShiftLock then
			local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if cam then
				targetYaw = M.atan2(-cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, -cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			end
		elseif moveDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			targetYaw = M.atan2(-moveDir.X, -moveDir.Z)
		end

		currentYaw = lerpAngle(currentYaw, targetYaw, dt * (isShiftLock and 40 or 10))

		align.CFrame =
			ANG(0, currentYaw, 0) *
			ANG(tiltY + slopeTilt, 0, 0) *
			ANG(tiltX, 0, tiltZ) *
			ANG(idleX, idleY, idleZ)
	end)
end

if player.Character then
	task.spawn(setupCharacter, player.Character)
end

-- mesh avatar
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Proxy
local Connections = {}
local hasDied = false -- Tracks if the player has already died once

local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, connection in ipairs(Connections) do
		connection:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function Cleanup()
	DisconnectAll()
	if Proxy then
		Proxy:Destroy()
		Proxy = nil
	end
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

local function IsInvisibleActivator(part)
	return part
		and part:IsA("BasePart")
		and part.Name == "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
		and part.Transparency == 1
end

local function IsInsideActivator(part, activator)
	if not IsInvisibleActivator(activator) then
		return false
	end

	local relative = activator.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(part.Position)  
	local halfSize = activator.Size / 2  
	local limbHalfSize = part.Size / 2  

	return math.abs(relative.X) <= halfSize.X + limbHalfSize.X  
		and math.abs(relative.Y) <= halfSize.Y + limbHalfSize.Y  
		and math.abs(relative.Z) <= halfSize.Z + limbHalfSize.Z
end

local function UpdateVisibility(part, activator)
	if not part then return end

	if activator and IsInsideActivator(part, activator) then  
		part.Transparency = 1  
	else  
		part.Transparency = 0  
	end
end

local function ApplyProxy(char)
	if hasDied then return end -- Do not run if player has died before

	Cleanup()

	local Root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)  
	local Head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)  
	local Humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

	if not Root or not Head or not Humanoid then return end  

	-- Stop permanent proxy generation as soon as the character dies
	Humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		hasDied = true
		Cleanup()
	end)

	local LeftArm = char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")  
	local RightArm = char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")  
	local LeftLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")  
	local RightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")  

	Proxy = Instance.new("Model")  
	Proxy.Name = "LocalVisualAvatar"  
	Proxy.Parent = workspace  

	local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))  
	local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Vector3.new(2, 1.2000000476837158, 1.2999999523162842))  

	local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)  
	local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)  
	local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)  
	local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)  

	HideCharacter(char)  

	table.insert(Connections, char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)  
		if not hasDied then HideObject(object) end
	end))  

	table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()  
		if not char.Parent or hasDied then  
			Cleanup()  
			return  
		end  

		HideCharacter(char)  

		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Root.CFrame  
		ProxyHead.CFrame = Head.CFrame  

		if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = LeftArm.CFrame end  
		if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = RightArm.CFrame end  
		if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = LeftLeg.CFrame end  
		if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = RightLeg.CFrame end  

		local Activator = workspace:FindFirstChild("INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE")  

		if Activator and IsInvisibleActivator(Activator) then  
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyTorso, Activator)
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyHead, Activator)  
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyLeftArm, Activator)  
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyRightArm, Activator)  
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyLeftLeg, Activator)  
			UpdateVisibility(ProxyRightLeg, Activator)  
		else  
			if ProxyTorso then ProxyTorso.Transparency = 0 end
			if ProxyHead then ProxyHead.Transparency = 0 end  
			if ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.Transparency = 0 end  
			if ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.Transparency = 0 end  
			if ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.Transparency = 0 end  
			if ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.Transparency = 0 end  
		end  
	end))
end

Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
	if hasDied then return end
	task.wait(0.5)
	ApplyProxy(char)
end)

if Player.Character then
	task.wait(0.5)
	ApplyProxy(Player.Character)
end
Wait(0.2)
--spawn animation
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")

local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")

local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

	CFrame.new(102.404160,440.754608,46.640419,0.954303,0.003310,0.298821,-0.002969,0.999994,-0.001596,-0.298825,0.000636,0.954308)

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

    for _, config in ipairs(animations) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)

-- idle and walk
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
	Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function SetLayer(track, layer)
	layer = math.clamp(layer, 1, #PriorityLayers)
	track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layer]
end

local walkConfigs = {
	{ id = "rbxassetid://204328711", layer = 2, speed = 1, startPos = 0 },
	{ id = "rbxassetid://214748382", layer = 2, speed = 0.66, startPos = 0 },
	{ id = "rbxassetid://241279220", layer = 3, speed = 0, startPos = 0.8 },
	{ id = "rbxassetid://126753849", layer = 5, speed = 0, startPos = 0.4 }
}

local idleConfigs = {
	{ id = "rbxassetid://203929811", layer = 3, speed = 1, startPos = 0 },
	{ id = "rbxassetid://21633130", layer = 2, speed = 1, startPos = 0 },
	{ id = "rbxassetid://126753849", layer = 3, speed = 0, startPos = 0.4 }
}

local function loadTracks(configs)
	local loadedTracks = {}
	for _, config in ipairs(configs) do
		local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		anim.AnimationId = config.id

		local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
		SetLayer(track, config.layer)

		table.insert(loadedTracks, {
			track = track,
			speed = config.speed,
			startPos = config.startPos,
			activeState = false -- Track desired play state manually
		})
	end
	return loadedTracks
end

local walkTracks = loadTracks(walkConfigs)
local idleTracks = loadTracks(idleConfigs)

local fadeTime = 0.35
local isAlive = true
local connections = {}

local function isBlockActive()
	local block = Workspace:FindFirstChild("INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE")
	if block and block:IsA("BasePart") then
		local isBlack = (block.Color == Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)) or (block.BrickColor == BrickColor.new("Black"))
		if isBlack and block.Transparency == 1 then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function stopTracks(trackList)
	for _, item in ipairs(trackList) do
		item.activeState = false
		item.track:Stop(fadeTime)
	end
end

local function killScript()
	if not isAlive then return end
	isAlive = false

	for _, conn in ipairs(connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(connections)

	stopTracks(walkTracks)
	stopTracks(idleTracks)
end

local currentState = nil -- Keeps track of "WALK", "IDLE", or "NONE"

local function updateAnimations()
	if not isAlive or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		killScript()
		return
	end

	if isBlockActive() then
		if currentState ~= "NONE" then
			currentState = "NONE"
			stopTracks(walkTracks)
			stopTracks(idleTracks)
		end
		return
	end

	local isMoving = humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0
	local isGrounded = humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed 
		or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running 
		or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics

	local targetState = "NONE"
	if isGrounded then
		targetState = isMoving and "WALK" or "IDLE"
	end

	-- Only run state changes if state actually shifted
	if targetState == currentState then return end
	currentState = targetState

	if targetState == "WALK" then
		stopTracks(idleTracks)
		for _, item in ipairs(walkTracks) do
			item.activeState = true
			item.track:Play(fadeTime)
			item.track.TimePosition = item.startPos
			item.track:AdjustSpeed(item.speed)
		end
	elseif targetState == "IDLE" then
		stopTracks(walkTracks)
		for _, item in ipairs(idleTracks) do
			item.activeState = true
			item.track:Play(fadeTime)
			item.track.TimePosition = item.startPos
			item.track:AdjustSpeed(item.speed)
		end
	else
		stopTracks(walkTracks)
		stopTracks(idleTracks)
	end
end

-- Event Listeners
table.insert(connections, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MoveDirection"):Connect(updateAnimations))
table.insert(connections, humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(updateAnimations))
table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(killScript))

table.insert(connections, Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE" then updateAnimations() end
end))

table.insert(connections, Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE" then updateAnimations() end
end))

task.spawn(function()
	while isAlive do
		updateAnimations()
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end)

updateAnimations()
Wait(2)

local function Skill1Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

	CFrame.new(134.429764,441.071442,45.617096,-0.682456,-0.477861,0.553084,0.017341,0.745891,0.665843,-0.730721,0.464000,-0.500751)
}

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 248335946, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.1, speed = 0,    duration = 1.0 },
        { id = 70989227,  layer = 4, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1,    duration = 1.0 },
        { id = 21417802,  layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 1.0, speed = 0,    duration = 1.0 },
        { id = 287325678, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.0, speed = 0,    duration = 1.0 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1)

    local batch2 = {
        { id = 94190213,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 1.0, speed = 0.10, duration = 1.8 },
        { id = 242455877, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.0, speed = 0.34, duration = 1.8 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch2) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

task.spawn(function()
	local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local RootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	local FORWARD_DISTANCE = 15
	local UP_DISTANCE = 20
	local MOVE_DURATION = 0.8
	local AIR_DURATION = 0.4
	local PREP_DURATION = 1

	Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 9

	task.wait(PREP_DURATION)

	local OldGravity = workspace.Gravity
	local OldAnchored = RootPart.Anchored
	local OldAutoRotate = Humanoid.AutoRotate
	local OldSit = Humanoid.Sit

	local StartCFrame = RootPart.CFrame

	local Forward = StartCFrame.LookVector

	local TargetPosition =
		StartCFrame.Position
		+ Forward * FORWARD_DISTANCE
		+ Vector3.new(0, UP_DISTANCE, 0)

	local TargetCFrame =
		CFrame.new(TargetPosition) * StartCFrame.Rotation

	Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
	Humanoid.Sit = true

	workspace.Gravity = 0
	RootPart.Anchored = true

	local StartTime = os.clock()

	while true do
		local Alpha = math.clamp(
			(os.clock() - StartTime) / MOVE_DURATION,
			0,
			1
		)

		Alpha = Alpha * Alpha * (3 - 2 * Alpha)

		RootPart.CFrame = StartCFrame:Lerp(TargetCFrame, Alpha)

		if Alpha >= 1 then
			break
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	RootPart.CFrame = TargetCFrame

	task.wait(AIR_DURATION)

	RootPart.Anchored = OldAnchored
	workspace.Gravity = OldGravity

	Humanoid.Sit = OldSit
	Humanoid.AutoRotate = OldAutoRotate
	Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
end)

end




local function Skill2Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

-- === 2. ROOT SEPARATION (Desync Setup) ===
local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")

local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

	CFrame.new(194.979080,440.603729,19.256046,-0.052310,-0.138648,-0.988959,-0.104468,0.985641,-0.132657,0.993152,0.096375,-0.066043)

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1     

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
Wait(0.05)
local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function SetLayer(track, layer)
    layer = math.clamp(layer, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layer]
end

local function playAnim(id, layer, fadeTime, speed, timePos, stopDelay)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)

    local track = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)
    SetLayer(track, layer)

    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(speed)

    if stopDelay then
        task.delay(stopDelay, function()
            track:Stop(fadeTime)
        end)
    end

    return track
end

task.spawn(function()
    playAnim(287325678, 2, 0.5, 1, 0, 1.5)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    playAnim(85837259, 2, 0.5, 0.5, 0, 1.5)
end)
end





local function Skill3Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

-- === 2. ROOT SEPARATION (Desync Setup) ===
local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

-- === 4. ANIMATION TIMELINE CONFIGURATION ===

	CFrame.new(54.985020,439.665009,6.485168,-0.916587,0.089742,-0.389633,-0.027203,0.958232,0.284696,0.398908,0.271548,-0.875862)

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local animations = {
        { id = 214748382, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.00, speed = 1.0, duration = 2.5 },
        { id = 126752874, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.50, speed = 0.5, duration = 2.0 },
        { id = 183294396, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.00, speed = 1.0, duration = 2.5 },
        { id = 85835312,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.45, speed = 0.4, duration = 3 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(animations) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

local function dashForward(duration, studsPerSecond)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	
	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then return end

	local elapsedTime = 0
	local connection
	
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		elapsedTime = elapsedTime + deltaTime
		
		if elapsedTime >= duration then
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		local cameraLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatDirection = Vector3.new(cameraLook.X, 0, cameraLook.Z)
		
		if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
		else
			flatDirection = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		local distanceThisFrame = studsPerSecond * deltaTime
		local currentPos = rootPart.Position
		local targetPos = currentPos + (flatDirection * distanceThisFrame)

		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatDirection)
	end)
end

dashForward(3, 18)
end






local function Skill4Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

-- === 2. ROOT SEPARATION (Desync Setup) ===
local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

-- === 4. ANIMATION TIMELINE CONFIGURATION ===
local frames = {
	CFrame.new(113.743454,440.754395,-16.587496,0.143509,-0.038670,-0.988893,-0.000000,0.999236,-0.039074,0.989649,0.005608,0.143400),

	CFrame.new(114.078751,439.595612,-17.021404,0.080521,0.906698,-0.414024,0.156106,0.398775,0.903664,0.984453,-0.137396,-0.109431),
	CFrame.new(114.079353,439.598419,-17.008673,0.077207,0.898737,-0.431637,0.152073,0.417248,0.895979,0.985349,-0.134816,-0.104459)

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 188242481, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1.0, duration = 2.0 },
        { id = 94190213,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.3, timePos = 1.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 2.5 },
        { id = 287325678, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1.0, duration = 2.8 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1.8)

    local batch2 = {
        { id = 85837259, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 0.6, speed = 0.5, duration = 1.8 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch2) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)
Wait(0.3)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

task.spawn(function()
	local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local RootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	local DISTANCE = 20
	local BASE_UP = 15
	local MOVE_DURATION = 2
	local AIR_DURATION = 0.3

	local OldGravity = workspace.Gravity
	local OldAnchored = RootPart.Anchored
	local OldAutoRotate = Humanoid.AutoRotate
	local OldSit = Humanoid.Sit

	local StartPosition = RootPart.Position

	Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
	Humanoid.Sit = true

	workspace.Gravity = 0
	RootPart.Anchored = true

	local StartTime = os.clock()

	while true do
		local Alpha = math.clamp(
			(os.clock() - StartTime) / MOVE_DURATION,
			0,
			1
		)

		local SmoothAlpha = Alpha * Alpha * (3 - 2 * Alpha)

		local CameraLook = Camera.CFrame.LookVector

		local HorizontalDirection = Vector3.new(
			CameraLook.X,
			0,
			CameraLook.Z
		)

		if HorizontalDirection.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			HorizontalDirection = HorizontalDirection.Unit
		else
			HorizontalDirection = Vector3.new(
				RootPart.CFrame.LookVector.X,
				0,
				RootPart.CFrame.LookVector.Z
			).Unit
		end

		local PitchInfluence = math.max(CameraLook.Y, 0)

		local HorizontalDistance =
			DISTANCE * (1 - PitchInfluence * 0.5)

		local UpwardDistance =
			BASE_UP + DISTANCE * PitchInfluence

		local TargetPosition =
			StartPosition
			+ HorizontalDirection * HorizontalDistance * SmoothAlpha
			+ Vector3.new(0, UpwardDistance * SmoothAlpha, 0)

		local LookDirection = HorizontalDirection

		RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
			TargetPosition,
			TargetPosition + LookDirection,
			Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
		)

		if Alpha >= 1 then
			break
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	task.wait(AIR_DURATION)

	RootPart.Anchored = OldAnchored
	workspace.Gravity = OldGravity

	Humanoid.Sit = OldSit
	Humanoid.AutoRotate = OldAutoRotate
end)

end

















local function UltimateSkill1Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

-- === 2. ROOT SEPARATION (Desync Setup) ===
local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

	CFrame.new(127.253143,440.738434,-28.083000,-0.742218,0.005611,0.670134,0.027006,0.999403,0.021542,-0.669614,0.034086,-0.741927),
	CFrame.new(127.229553,440.749084,-28.112514,-0.725620,0.001731,0.688093,0.009092,0.999934,0.007072,-0.688035,0.011388,-0.725588)

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- Helper function to check if the interrupt trigger exists in Workspace
local function checkInterrupt()
	local interruptPart = workspace:FindFirstChild("INTERRUPT_ACTIVATE", true)
	if interruptPart and interruptPart:IsA("BasePart") then
		return interruptPart.Transparency <= 0
	end
	return false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Check for interrupt signal
	local isInterrupted = checkInterrupt()

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	-- Terminate execution if animation ends OR if interrupt block is detected
	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames or isInterrupted then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 214748382, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1.0, duration = 1.0 },
        { id = 190075311, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1.1, duration = 1.0 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1.1)

    local batch2 = {
        { id = 248336294, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.60 },
        { id = 241279220, layer = 4, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.75 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch2) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)
-- movement
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

local function dashForward(duration, studsPerSecond)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	
	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then return end

	local elapsedTime = 0
	local connection
	
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		elapsedTime = elapsedTime + deltaTime
		
		if elapsedTime >= duration then
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		local cameraLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatDirection = Vector3.new(cameraLook.X, 0, cameraLook.Z)
		
		if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
		else
			flatDirection = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		local distanceThisFrame = studsPerSecond * deltaTime
		local currentPos = rootPart.Position
		local targetPos = currentPos + (flatDirection * distanceThisFrame)

		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatDirection)
	end)
end

dashForward(1.5, 30)
end








































local function UltimateSkill2Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

-- === 0. INVISIBLE ACTIVATE BLOCK SETUP ===
-- Creates the black block in workspace when the CFrame starts playing
local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

-- === 2. ROOT SEPARATION (Desync Setup) ===
local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

-- === 3. PROXY MODEL CREATION ===
DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

	CFrame.new(-227.255661,439.238251,-127.545029,-0.821129,0.359695,-0.443133,-0.043479,0.734736,0.676959,0.569084,0.575137,-0.587674)
