local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
Wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 188853932, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.3, speed = 0, duration = 1.0 },
        { id = 243663079, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0, duration = 3.0 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1)

    local batch2 = {
        { id = 126753849, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.0, speed = 0.6, duration = 3.0 },
        { id = 21633130,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.3, speed = 0.0, duration = 9.5 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch2) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(3)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 116690317, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.4, speed = 0, duration = 4.0 })

    task.wait(1.2)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 188854226, layer = 4, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.4, speed = 0, duration = 1.0 })

    task.wait(1)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 85836344, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.0, speed = 0.6, duration = 2.0 })

    task.wait(1.5)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 188854226, layer = 4, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.4, speed = 0, duration = 2.5 })
end)
Wait(0.8)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

local function dashForward(duration, studsPerSecond)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	
	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then return end

	local elapsedTime = 0
	local connection
	
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		elapsedTime = elapsedTime + deltaTime
		
		if elapsedTime >= duration then
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		local cameraLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatDirection = Vector3.new(cameraLook.X, 0, cameraLook.Z)
		
		if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
		else
			flatDirection = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		local distanceThisFrame = studsPerSecond * deltaTime
		local currentPos = rootPart.Position
		local targetPos = currentPos + (flatDirection * distanceThisFrame)

		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatDirection)
	end)
end

-- Run the entire sequence in a background thread so it won't block other code below it
task.spawn(function()
	dashForward(3.5, 15)
	task.wait(3.5)

	dashForward(1.5, 5)
	task.wait(2)

	dashForward(0.5, 30)
	task.wait(0.5)

	dashForward(1.2, 50)
	task.wait(1.2)

	dashForward(1.2, 20)
	task.wait(1.2)

	dashForward(1.2, -35)
end)
end






























local function UltimateSkill3Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

	CFrame.new(185.857468,440.385376,20.917736,0.455413,-0.313550,0.833238,0.060961,0.944714,0.322180,-0.888191,-0.095930,0.449349)

local targetFPS = 55
local playbackSpeed = 1

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	HideCharacter(Character)

	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	if Torso then
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 218504594, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.50, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 4.8 },
        { id = 85837259,  layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.50, timePos = 0.0, speed = 1.0, duration = 1.8 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1)

    local batch2 = {
        { id = 214748382, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.50, timePos = 0.0, speed = 0.8, duration = 3.0 },
        { id = 94190213,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 1.0, speed = 0.0, duration = 3.0 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch2) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(3)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 21633130, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.50, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 1.0 })
end)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

local function dashForward(duration, studsPerSecond)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	
	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then return end

	local elapsedTime = 0
	local connection
	
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		elapsedTime = elapsedTime + deltaTime
		
		if elapsedTime >= duration then
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		local cameraLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatDirection = Vector3.new(cameraLook.X, 0, cameraLook.Z)
		
		if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
		else
			flatDirection = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		local distanceThisFrame = studsPerSecond * deltaTime
		local currentPos = rootPart.Position
		local targetPos = currentPos + (flatDirection * distanceThisFrame)

		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatDirection)
	end)
end

dashForward(4.5, 18)

end












local function UltimateSkill4Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

local targetFPS = 55
local playbackSpeed = 0.8

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	HideCharacter(Character)

	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	if Torso then
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local animations = {
        { id = 94190213,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 1.0, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.9 },
        { id = 96621537,  layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 3.0, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.9 },
        { id = 241279220, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.9 },
        { id = 204328711, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.5, timePos = 0.6, speed = 0.0, duration = 0.9 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(animations) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

local function dashForward(duration, studsPerSecond)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	
	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then return end

	local elapsedTime = 0
	local connection
	
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
		elapsedTime = elapsedTime + deltaTime
		
		if elapsedTime >= duration then
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		local cameraLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flatDirection = Vector3.new(cameraLook.X, 0, cameraLook.Z)
		
		if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
		else
			flatDirection = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		local distanceThisFrame = studsPerSecond * deltaTime
		local currentPos = rootPart.Position
		local targetPos = currentPos + (flatDirection * distanceThisFrame)

		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatDirection)
	end)
end

dashForward(0.8, 15)
end












local function UltimateFunction()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) -- Black
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1 -- 0% transparent (Fully Opaque)
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false

invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false

-- === 1. INVISIBILITY HELPERS ===
local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

	CFrame.new(227.623337,440.439178,-5.567240,0.391109,-0.055570,0.918665,-0.002759,0.998100,0.061550,-0.920340,-0.026607,0.390212),
	CFrame.new(227.619873,440.442871,-5.562160,0.382444,-0.055071,0.922336,-0.003014,0.998143,0.060847,-0.923974,-0.026050,0.381568),
	CFrame.new(227.611786,440.451538,-5.550304,0.362096,-0.053886,0.930582,-0.003583,0.998240,0.059198,-0.932134,-0.024770,0.361266)
}

local targetFPS = 55        
local playbackSpeed = 1    

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

-- === 5. COMBINED RUNTIME LOOP ===
local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	-- Maintain local character invisibility
	HideCharacter(Character)

	-- Keep the block anchored at your current dynamic root position
	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	-- Attach the gray Torso and all limbs relative to your real animated Torso
	if Torso then 
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		-- Clean up desync setup
		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		-- Destroy the INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE block when playback stops
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	-- Update desynced root position
	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	local character = player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if humanoid then
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0.5

		task.delay(4, function()
			if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
			end
		end)
	end
end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local batch1 = {
        { id = 243663079, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0, duration = 4 },
        { id = 184573779, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.5, speed = 0, duration = 1 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(batch1) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end

    task.wait(1)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 184573748, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.8, speed = 0, duration = 1 })

    task.wait(1)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 54504034, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.30, timePos = 0.6, speed = 0.6, duration = 1 })

    task.wait(1)

    playCustomAnimation({ id = 248263260, layer = 3, fadeTime = 0.35, timePos = 0.2, speed = 0, duration = 1 })
end)

end








local function Punch1Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

local targetFPS = 55
local playbackSpeed = 1

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	HideCharacter(Character)

	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	if Torso then
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))

local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer

local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function SetLayer(track, layer)
    layer = math.clamp(layer, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layer]
end
local layer = 2
local animation = Instance.new("Animation")

local animationTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)

animationTrack:Play(fadeTime)
animationTrack.TimePosition = 3
animationTrack:AdjustSpeed(-0.5)
task.delay(0.35, function()
    animationTrack:Stop(fadeTime)
end)

end







local function Punch2Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil

if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

local frames = {

	CFrame.new(112.054924,440.863831,235.230072,0.938973,0.240541,-0.245907,-0.120872,0.899991,0.418815,0.322057,-0.363532,0.874142),
	CFrame.new(112.050156,440.873199,235.232407,0.943976,0.234866,-0.231837,-0.135244,0.916118,0.377408,0.301030,-0.324909,0.896557),
	CFrame.new(112.044197,440.884949,235.235306,0.949851,0.228701,-0.213258,-0.151945,0.933616,0.324459,0.273305,-0.275784,0.921546)
}

local targetFPS = 55
local playbackSpeed = 1

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	HideCharacter(Character)

	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	if Torso then
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function SetLayer(track, layer)
    layer = math.clamp(layer, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layer]
end
local layer = 2 
local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
animation.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?version=1&id=188853932"
local animationTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)
SetLayer(animationTrack, layer)
local fadeTime = 0.2
animationTrack:Play(fadeTime)
animationTrack.TimePosition = 0.05
animationTrack:AdjustSpeed(1)
task.delay(0.6, function()
    animationTrack:Stop(fadeTime)
end)
end

		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function SetLayer(track, layer)
    layer = math.clamp(layer, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layer]
end

local layer = 2

local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
animation.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?version=1&id=181437419"
local animationTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)
SetLayer(animationTrack, layer)
local fadeTime = 0.15
animationTrack:Play(fadeTime)
animationTrack.TimePosition = 0.5
animationTrack:AdjustSpeed(0)
task.delay(0.6, function()
    animationTrack:Stop(fadeTime)
end)

end








local function Punch4Function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local Hum = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local Hrp0 = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local Proxy
local Connections = {}

local invisibleBlock = Instance.new("Part")
invisibleBlock.Name = "INVISIBLE_ACTIVATE"
invisibleBlock.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
invisibleBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
invisibleBlock.Transparency = 1
invisibleBlock.Anchored = true
invisibleBlock.CanCollide = false
invisibleBlock.CanTouch = false
invisibleBlock.CanQuery = false
invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
invisibleBlock.Parent = workspace

local function DisconnectAll()
	for _, conn in ipairs(Connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(Connections)
end

local function HideObject(object)
	if object:IsA("BasePart") then
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
		object.Transparency = 1
	elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
		or object:IsA("Trail")
		or object:IsA("Beam")
		or object:IsA("Smoke")
		or object:IsA("Fire")
		or object:IsA("Sparkles") then
		object.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function HideCharacter(char)
	for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		HideObject(object)
	end
end

local function MakePart(name, size)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = name
	part.Size = size
	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
	part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.Parent = Proxy
	return part
end

local Hrp1 = Hrp0:Clone()

Character.Parent = nil
Hrp0.Parent = Hrp1

if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
	Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = nil
end

Hrp1.Parent = Character
Character.Parent = workspace

Hum.PlatformStand = false
Hum.Sit = false
Hum.AutoRotate = true

DisconnectAll()
if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end

Proxy = Instance.new("Model")
Proxy.Name = "GREYMODEL"
Proxy.Parent = workspace

local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm")
local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")

local ProxyTorso = MakePart("Torso", Torso and Torso.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
local ProxyHead = MakePart("Head", Head and Head.Size or Vector3.new(2, 1.2, 1.3))

local ProxyLeftArm = LeftArm and MakePart("LeftArm", LeftArm.Size)
local ProxyRightArm = RightArm and MakePart("RightArm", RightArm.Size)
local ProxyLeftLeg = LeftLeg and MakePart("LeftLeg", LeftLeg.Size)
local ProxyRightLeg = RightLeg and MakePart("RightLeg", RightLeg.Size)

HideCharacter(Character)

table.insert(Connections, Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	HideObject(object)
end))

local targetFPS = 55
local playbackSpeed = 1

local totalFrames = #frames
local totalDuration = ((totalFrames - 1) / targetFPS) / playbackSpeed
local origin = frames[1]:Inverse()

local function calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)
	if elapsedTime >= totalDuration then
		return totalFrames, 1, true
	end
	
	local progress = elapsedTime / totalDuration
	local scaledIndex = progress * (totalFrames - 1) + 1
	local index = math.floor(scaledIndex)
	local alpha = scaledIndex - index
	
	return index, alpha, false
end

local startTime = tick()
local finished = false

table.insert(Connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	HideCharacter(Character)

	if invisibleBlock and invisibleBlock.Parent then
		invisibleBlock.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
	end

	if Torso then
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Torso.CFrame)
	else
		ProxyTorso.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame
	end

	if Head then ProxyHead.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * Head.CFrame) end
	if LeftArm and ProxyLeftArm then ProxyLeftArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftArm.CFrame) end
	if RightArm and ProxyRightArm then ProxyRightArm.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightArm.CFrame) end
	if LeftLeg and ProxyLeftLeg then ProxyLeftLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * LeftLeg.CFrame) end
	if RightLeg and ProxyRightLeg then ProxyRightLeg.CFrame = Hrp0.CFrame * (Hrp1.CFrame:Inverse() * RightLeg.CFrame) end

	if finished then return end

	local currentTime = tick()
	local elapsedTime = currentTime - startTime
	local index, alpha, isFinished = calculateMainTimeline(elapsedTime)

	if isFinished or index >= totalFrames then
		finished = true

		Hrp0.CFrame = Hrp1.CFrame
		Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity

		if Hrp0:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") then
			Hrp0.RootJoint.Part0 = Hrp0
		end
		Hrp0.Parent = Character
		Hrp1:Destroy()

		if Proxy then Proxy:Destroy() end
		
		if invisibleBlock then
			invisibleBlock:Destroy()
		end

		DisconnectAll()
		return
	end

	local nextIndex = math.min(index + 1, totalFrames)
	local offsetA = origin * frames[index]
	local offsetB = origin * frames[nextIndex]
	
	local targetA = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetA
	local targetB = Hrp1.CFrame * offsetB

	Hrp0.CFrame = targetA:Lerp(targetB, alpha)
	Hrp0.Velocity = Hrp1.Velocity
end))
wait(0.05)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local PriorityLayers = {
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Core,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3,
    Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
}

local function playCustomAnimation(config)
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.id)

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
    
    local layerIndex = math.clamp(config.layer or 1, 1, #PriorityLayers)
    track.Priority = PriorityLayers[layerIndex]

    local fadeTime = config.fadeTime or 0.3
    track:Play(fadeTime)
    track.TimePosition = config.timePos or 0
    track:AdjustSpeed(config.speed or 1)

    task.delay(config.duration or 1, function()
        track:Stop(fadeTime)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local animations = {
        { id = 214748382, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.25, timePos = 0.65, speed = 0.1, duration = 0.6 },
        { id = 248335946, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.25, timePos = 0.60, speed = 0.1, duration = 0.6 },
        { id = 204328711, layer = 2, fadeTime = 0.25, timePos = 0.80, speed = 0.3, duration = 0.6 },
    }

    for _, config in ipairs(animations) do
        playCustomAnimation(config)
    end
end)

end

local function addCorner(instance, radius)
	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
	corner.Parent = instance
	return corner
end


local function addShadow(parent, expand)
	local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	shadow.Name = "Shadow"
	shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	shadow.Image = SHADOW_IMAGE
	shadow.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	shadow.ImageTransparency = 0.4
	shadow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	shadow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(24, 24, 276, 276)
	shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, expand, 1, expand)
	shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	shadow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	shadow.ZIndex = 0
	shadow.Parent = parent
	return shadow
end


local function setupSkillFrame(frame)
	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = SKILL_BASE_COLOR
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = SKILL_TRANSPARENCY
	frame.ClipsDescendants = true

	addCorner(frame, 12)
	addShadow(frame, 10)

	local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, SKILL_GRADIENT_TOP),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, SKILL_GRADIENT_BOTTOM)
	})
	gradient.Rotation = 90
	gradient.Parent = frame

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Name = "StatusStroke"
	stroke.Color = SKILL_ACCENT
	stroke.Thickness = 1.5
	stroke.Transparency = 0.2
	stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	stroke.Parent = frame

	return stroke
end

	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = ULTIMATE_GRADIENT_BOTTOM

	addCorner(frame, 10)

	local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, ULTIMATE_GRADIENT_TOP),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, ULTIMATE_GRADIENT_BOTTOM)
	})

	gradient.Parent = frame

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Name = "StatusStroke"
	stroke.Color = ULTIMATE_ACCENT
	stroke.Thickness = 2
	stroke.Transparency = 0
	stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	stroke.Parent = frame

	return stroke
end

		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, PUNCH_GRADIENT_BOTTOM)

local function createButton(parent, name)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")

	button.Name = name
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	button.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	button.Text = ""
	button.TextTransparency = 1
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.ZIndex = 3
	button.Parent = parent

	return button
end


local function createNameLabel(parent, name, textSize)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	label.Name = "SkillName"
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8)
	label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
	label.Text = name
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 242, 250)
	label.TextTransparency = 0
	label.TextScaled = textSize == nil
	label.TextSize = textSize or 14
	label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7
	label.ZIndex = 2
	label.Parent = parent

	return label
end

LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"] = Instance.new("Frame")
LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"].Name = "PunchPipsHolder"
LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"].Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"].Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 88)
LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"].BackgroundTransparency = 1
LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"].Parent = LMG2L["PunchContainer"]

local punchPipsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
punchPipsLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
punchPipsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
punchPipsLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center

local punchPips = {}

for i = 1, 4 do
	local pip = Instance.new("Frame")
	pip.Name = "Pip" .. i
	pip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
	pip.LayoutOrder = i
	pip.BackgroundColor3 = PUNCH_PIP_INACTIVE
	pip.BorderSizePixel = 0
	pip.Parent = LMG2L["PunchPipsHolder"]
	addCorner(pip, 1000)
	punchPips[i] = pip
end


local skillFrames = {
	LMG2L["Frame_2"],
	LMG2L["Frame_3"],
	LMG2L["Frame_4"],
	LMG2L["Frame_5"]
}

local skillStrokes = {
	stroke1,
	stroke2,
	stroke3,
	stroke4
}

local skillLabels = {
	SkillLabel1,
	SkillLabel2,
	SkillLabel3,
	SkillLabel4
}

local skillButtons = {
	SkillButton1,
	SkillButton2,
	SkillButton3,
	SkillButton4
}

local normalNames = {
	SKILL_1_NAME,
	SKILL_2_NAME,
	SKILL_3_NAME,
	SKILL_4_NAME
}

local ultimateNames = {
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_1_NAME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_2_NAME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_3_NAME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_4_NAME
}

local normalCooldowns = {
	SKILL_1_COOLDOWN,
	SKILL_2_COOLDOWN,
	SKILL_3_COOLDOWN,
	SKILL_4_COOLDOWN
}

local ultimateSkillCooldowns = {
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_1_COOLDOWN,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_2_COOLDOWN,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_3_COOLDOWN,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_4_COOLDOWN
}

local normalBusyTimes = {
	SKILL_1_BUSY_TIME,
	SKILL_2_BUSY_TIME,
	SKILL_3_BUSY_TIME,
	SKILL_4_BUSY_TIME
}

local ultimateSkillBusyTimes = {
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_1_BUSY_TIME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_2_BUSY_TIME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_3_BUSY_TIME,
	ULTIMATE_SKILL_4_BUSY_TIME
}

local punchFunctions = {
	Punch1Function,
	Punch2Function,
	Punch3Function,
	Punch4Function
}

local punchBusyTimes = {
	PUNCH_1_BUSY_TIME,
	PUNCH_2_BUSY_TIME,
	PUNCH_3_BUSY_TIME,
	PUNCH_4_BUSY_TIME
}

local skillOnCooldown = {
	false,
	false,
	false,
	false
}

local ultimateSkillOnCooldown = {
	false,
	false,
	false,
	false
}

local cooldownTokens = {
	0,
	0,
	0,
	0
}

local ultimateCooldownToken = 0

local globalSkillLock = false
local ultimateActive = false
local ultimateOnCooldown = false

local punchCombo = 1
local punchOnCooldown = false
local punchResetToken = 0


local function setStrokeState(index, onCooldown)
	local stroke = skillStrokes[index]

	if onCooldown then
		stroke.Color = COOLDOWN_STROKE_COLOR
		stroke.Transparency = 0.5
	else
		stroke.Color = ultimateActive and ULTIMATE_ACCENT or SKILL_ACCENT
		stroke.Transparency = 0.2
	end
end


local function flashFrame(frame, restoreTransparency)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = PRESSED_TRANSPARENCY

	task.wait()

	frame.BackgroundTransparency = restoreTransparency
end


local function updatePunchPips(comboStep, onCooldown)
	for i = 1, 4 do
		if onCooldown then
			punchPips[i].BackgroundColor3 = PUNCH_PIP_ACTIVE
		elseif i < comboStep then
			punchPips[i].BackgroundColor3 = PUNCH_PIP_ACTIVE
		else
			punchPips[i].BackgroundColor3 = PUNCH_PIP_INACTIVE
		end
	end
end


local function setAllButtonsEnabled(enabled)
	for _, button in ipairs(skillButtons) do
		button.Active = enabled
		button.Selectable = enabled
	end

	UltimateButton.Active = enabled
	UltimateButton.Selectable = enabled

	PunchButton.Active = enabled
	PunchButton.Selectable = enabled
end


local function startSkillCooldown(index, duration, label)
	cooldownTokens[index] += 1

	local myToken = cooldownTokens[index]

	setStrokeState(index, true)

	task.spawn(function()
		for remaining = duration, 1, -1 do
			if cooldownTokens[index] ~= myToken then
				return
			end

			label.Text = tostring(remaining)

			task.wait(1)
		end

		if cooldownTokens[index] ~= myToken then
			return
		end

		if ultimateActive then
			label.Text = ultimateNames[index]
			ultimateSkillOnCooldown[index] = false
		else
			label.Text = normalNames[index]
			skillOnCooldown[index] = false
		end

		setStrokeState(index, false)
	end)
end


local function resetAllSkillCooldowns()
	for i = 1, 4 do
		cooldownTokens[i] += 1

		skillOnCooldown[i] = false
		ultimateSkillOnCooldown[i] = false

		setStrokeState(i, false)

		if ultimateActive then
			skillLabels[i].Text = ultimateNames[i]
		else
			skillLabels[i].Text = normalNames[i]
		end
	end
end


local function executeSkill(index, normalFunction, ultimateFunction)
	if globalSkillLock then
		return
	end

	if ultimateActive then
		if ultimateSkillOnCooldown[index] then
			return
		end
	else
		if skillOnCooldown[index] then
			return
		end
	end

	globalSkillLock = true
	setAllButtonsEnabled(false)

	flashFrame(skillFrames[index], SKILL_TRANSPARENCY)

	local success
	local errorMessage
	local busyTime

	if ultimateActive then
		ultimateSkillOnCooldown[index] = true

		success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
			ultimateFunction()
		end)

		startSkillCooldown(
			index,
			ultimateSkillCooldowns[index],
			skillLabels[index]
		)

		busyTime = ultimateSkillBusyTimes[index]
	else
		skillOnCooldown[index] = true

		success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
			normalFunction()
		end)

		startSkillCooldown(
			index,
			normalCooldowns[index],
			skillLabels[index]
		)

		busyTime = normalBusyTimes[index]
	end

	if not success then
		warn("Skill error:", errorMessage)
	end

	if busyTime > 0 then
		task.wait(busyTime)
	end

	globalSkillLock = false
	setAllButtonsEnabled(true)
end


local function activateUltimate()
	if globalSkillLock then
		return
	end

	if ultimateOnCooldown then
		return
	end

	globalSkillLock = true
	setAllButtonsEnabled(false)

	flashFrame(LMG2L["Frame_6"], ULTIMATE_TRANSPARENCY)

	ultimateOnCooldown = true
	ultimateActive = true

	resetAllSkillCooldowns()

	for i = 1, 4 do
		skillLabels[i].Text = ultimateNames[i]
	end

	local success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
		UltimateFunction()
	end)

	if not success then
		warn("Ultimate error:", errorMessage)
	end

	if ULTIMATE_BUSY_TIME > 0 then
		task.wait(ULTIMATE_BUSY_TIME)
	end

	globalSkillLock = false
	setAllButtonsEnabled(true)

	task.delay(ULTIMATE_DURATION, function()
		ultimateActive = false

		for i = 1, 4 do
			cooldownTokens[i] += 1

			skillOnCooldown[i] = false
			ultimateSkillOnCooldown[i] = false

			skillLabels[i].Text = normalNames[i]
			setStrokeState(i, false)
		end
	end)

	ultimateCooldownToken += 1

	local myUltimateToken = ultimateCooldownToken

	task.spawn(function()
		for remaining = ULTIMATE_COOLDOWN, 1, -1 do
			if ultimateCooldownToken ~= myUltimateToken then
				return
			end

			UltimateLabel.Text = tostring(remaining)

			task.wait(1)
		end

		if ultimateCooldownToken ~= myUltimateToken then
			return
		end

		ultimateOnCooldown = false
		UltimateLabel.Text = ULTIMATE_NAME
	end)
end


local function executePunch()
	if globalSkillLock then
		return
	end

	if punchOnCooldown then
		return
	end

	globalSkillLock = true
	setAllButtonsEnabled(false)

	flashFrame(LMG2L["PunchFrame"], PUNCH_TRANSPARENCY)

	local index = punchCombo
	local success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
		punchFunctions[index]()
	end)

	if not success then
		warn("Punch error:", errorMessage)
	end

	local busyTime = punchBusyTimes[index]

	if busyTime > 0 then
		task.wait(busyTime)
	end

	if index == 4 then
		punchOnCooldown = true
		punchCombo = 1
		punchResetToken += 1

		updatePunchPips(1, true)

		task.delay(PUNCH_COMBO_COOLDOWN, function()
			punchOnCooldown = false
			updatePunchPips(1, false)
		end)
	else
		punchCombo = index + 1
		updatePunchPips(punchCombo, false)

		punchResetToken += 1

		local myResetToken = punchResetToken

		task.delay(PUNCH_RESET_TIME, function()
			if punchResetToken ~= myResetToken then
				return
			end

			punchCombo = 1
			updatePunchPips(1, false)
		end)
	end

	globalSkillLock = false
	setAllButtonsEnabled(true)
end


SkillButton1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	executeSkill(
		1,
		Skill1Function,
		UltimateSkill1Function
	)
end)


SkillButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	executeSkill(
		2,
		Skill2Function,
		UltimateSkill2Function
	)
end)


SkillButton3.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	executeSkill(
		3,
		Skill3Function,
		UltimateSkill3Function
	)
end)


SkillButton4.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	executeSkill(
		4,
		Skill4Function,
		UltimateSkill4Function
	)
end)


UltimateButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	activateUltimate()
end)


local punchHoldToken = 0

local function punchHoldLoop(myToken)
	while punchHoldToken == myToken do
		executePunch()
		task.wait()
	end
end

PunchButton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
	punchHoldToken += 1

	local myToken = punchHoldToken

	task.spawn(function()
		punchHoldLoop(myToken)
	end)
end)

PunchButton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
	punchHoldToken += 1
end)

PunchButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
	punchHoldToken += 1
end)


local function onCharacterAdded(character)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		LMG2L["ScreenGui_1"]:Destroy()
	end)
end


if LocalPlayer.Character then
	onCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character)
end

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)


return LMG2L["ScreenGui_1"], require
